  exceed the width of columns they are put into when resizing window.

  are children of the grid that you want to resize with grid.

<script>
$(document).ready(function(){

              
 window.getCookie = function(c_name) {
    var c_value = document.cookie,
        c_start = c_value.indexOf(" " + c_name + "=");
    if (c_start == -1) c_start = c_value.indexOf(c_name + "=");
    if (c_start == -1) {
        c_value = null;
    } else {
        c_start = c_value.indexOf("=", c_start) + 1;
        var c_end = c_value.indexOf(";", c_start);
        if (c_end == -1) {
            c_end = c_value.length;
        }
        c_value = unescape(c_value.substring(c_start, c_end));
    }
    return c_value;
} 
function product2(){
  $.getJSON('/cart.js', function(cart) {
    var cartItems2 = cart.items,
    correctProduct2 = 0,
    qtyInTheCart2 = 0,
    cartUpdates2 = {};
    for (var i=0; i<cartItems2.length; i++) {
      if (cartItems2[i].id === 13592298881099) {
        correctProduct2 = correctProduct2 + cartItems2[i].quantity;
      }
      if ( (cartItems2[i].id === 13836615974987) || (cartItems2[i].id === 13836616007755) || (cartItems2[i].id === 13592298913867) ){
        correctProduct2 = correctProduct2 + cartItems2[i].quantity +3;
      }
    }
    var ABcookie = getCookie("ab-cookie");
    if (!ABcookie) {
      for (var i=0; i<cartItems2.length; i++) {
        if ( cartItems2[i].id === 29916450881611 ){
            qtyInTheCart2 = cartItems2[i].quantity;
            break;
            }
       }
       if ( ( correctProduct2 < 3 ) && ( qtyInTheCart2 > 0 ) ) {
          cartUpdates2 = { 29916450881611: 0 }
        }
        else if ( ( correctProduct2 >= 3 ) && ( qtyInTheCart2 !== 1 ) ) {
          cartUpdates2 = { 29916450881611: 1 }
        }
          else {
            return;
          }
      } else{
      for (var i=0; i<cartItems2.length; i++) {
        if ( cartItems2[i].id === 28115022225 ){
            qtyInTheCart2 = cartItems2[i].quantity;
            break;
            }
       }
       if ( ( correctProduct2 < 3 ) && ( qtyInTheCart2 > 0 ) ) {
          cartUpdates2 = { 28115022225: 0 }
        }
        else if ( ( correctProduct2 >= 3 ) && ( qtyInTheCart2 !== 1 ) ) {
          cartUpdates2 = { 28115022225: 1 }
        }
          else {
            return;
          }
      
      }
    $.ajax({
      type: 'POST',
      url: '/cart/update.js',
      data: { updates: cartUpdates2 },
      dataType: 'json',
      cache: false,
      success: function() {
        $.getJSON("/cart.js", function(cart) {
          refreshCart(cart);
          if(window.location.href.indexOf("cart") > -1){
          location.reload();
          }
          return;
        });
      }
    });
  });

<script>
    function showRecoverForm(){
      $("#customer_login").fadeOut(300);
      $("#recover").delay(300).fadeIn(300);
    }
    function hideRecoverForm(){
      $("#customer_login").delay(300).fadeIn(300);
      $("#recover").fadeOut(300);
    }
  $(document).ready(function(){
    $(".sign-in").click(function(){
      $("#si-modal").removeClass("inactive").addClass("active");
    });
    $(".si-close,.si-bkg").click(function(){
      $("#si-modal").removeClass("active").delay(500).queue(function(next){
        $("#si-modal").addClass("inactive");
        next();
      });});
    $(".si-tab").click(function(){
      $(".si-tab.active").removeClass("active");
      $(this).addClass("active");
      if($(this).is("#si-register")){
        $("#forms").addClass("register");
      }else{
        $("#forms").removeClass("register");
      }
    });
    $("#si-register-button").click(function(){
      $(".si-tab.active").removeClass("active");
      $("#si-register").addClass("active");
      $("#forms").addClass("register");
    });
    $(".nav-item.sign-in").click(function(){
    	$("#top-nav").prop("checked",false);
    });
    $(".si-link").click(function(){
      window.scrollTo(0,0);
      $("#si-modal").removeClass("inactive").addClass("active");
    });
  });
</script>

<script>
    var StampedCarouselFn = (function (window, document) {
        var perSlide, autoPlay, autoPlaySpeed, autoPlayInterval, link;
        var $;
        var arrData = [];
        var isMobile = /iPhone|iPod|Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);

        function init(data, obj, jQuery) {
            $ = jQuery;
            perSlide = obj.data('per-slide');
            autoPlay = obj.data('auto-play') == true ? true : false;
            autoPlaySpeed = obj.data('auto-play-speed') || 10000;
            autoPlaySpeed = autoPlaySpeed < 1000 ? autoPlaySpeed * 1000 : autoPlaySpeed;
            var storeUrl = obj.attr('data-store-url');
            var showAvatar = obj.attr('data-show-avatar');
            var isTotalWidget = obj.data('is-widget-total') == true ? true : false;
            var isRandom = obj.data('random');
            var isForceRichSnippet = obj.attr('data-rich-snippet-force') == 'true' ? true : false;
            obj.attr('data-rating-all', data.ratingAll);

            var take = obj.data('take');

            var classNameWidget = '#stamped-reviews-widget[data-widget-type="carousel"]';
            var styleColorTitle = obj.data('style-color-title');
            var styleColorText = obj.data('style-color-text');
            var styleColorLink = obj.data('style-color-link');
            var styleColorStar = obj.data('style-color-star');

	        var isLazyLoad = false;
            if (window.LazyLoadStamped && LazyLoadStamped.update)
            {
		        isLazyLoad = true;
            }

            var style = '';

            if (styleColorTitle) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-title { color:' + styleColorTitle + ' !important; }';
            }
            if (styleColorText) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-text { color:' + styleColorText + ' !important; }';
            }
            if (styleColorLink) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-link { color:' + styleColorLink + ' !important; }';
            }
            if (styleColorStar) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-star i { color:' + styleColorStar + ' !important; }';
            }

            if (style) {
                $(obj).append('<style>' + style + '</style>');
            }

            link = obj.data('link');
            perSlide = !perSlide ? 3 : perSlide;
            arrData = data.data;


            if (isRandom) {
                arrData = shuffleArray(arrData);
            }


            if (arrData.length <= 0) {
                $(obj).hide();
                return;
            }

            var totalReviews = data.totalAllWithNPS;
            var totalRating = data.ratingAll;

            if (isTotalWidget == true) {
                totalReviews = data.total;
		        totalRating = data.rating;
            }

            if (isForceRichSnippet == true) {
                var is_root = location.pathname == "/"; //Equals true if we're at the root
                if (totalReviews > 0 && !is_root) {
                    // create richsnippet
                    var richSnippet = {
                        "@context": "http://schema.org",
                        "@type": "Product",
                        "aggregateRating": {
                            "@type": "AggregateRating",
                            "bestRating": "5",
                            "worstRating": "1",
                            "reviewCount": totalReviews,
                            "ratingValue": data.rating,
                            "itemReviewed": data.shop
                        },
                        "name": data.shop
                    };

                    var script = document.createElement('script');
                    script.type = 'application/ld+json';
                    script.innerHTML = JSON.stringify(richSnippet);
                    document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0].appendChild(script);
                }

            }

            var perWidth = Math.floor(100 / perSlide);

            var stringBlocks = '';
            var totalTake = take || 30;

            var totalLength = arrData.length >= totalTake ? totalTake : arrData.length;
            var currentSlide = 0;
            var currentWidth = 100;

            // carousel title
            var title = $(obj).attr('data-title');

            if (!!title) {
                $('.stamped-carousel-title', obj).html(title);
            }

            var starHtml = "";
	    var ratingRemain = 5;

            while (totalRating >= 0.01 || ratingRemain > 0) {
                if (totalRating >= 0.5 && totalRating < 1) {
                    starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star-half-o"></i>';
                } else if (totalRating >= 1){
                    starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star"></i>';
		} else {
                    starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star-o"></i>';
                }

                totalRating--;
ratingRemain--;
            }

            $('.stamped-carousel-stars-wrapper', obj).html('');
            $('.stamped-carousel-stars-wrapper', obj).html(starHtml);

            for (var i = 0; i < totalLength; i++) {

                currentSlide++;
                if (currentSlide == (perSlide)) {
                    perWidth = currentWidth;
                    currentWidth = 100;
                    currentSlide = 0;
                } else {
                    perWidth = Math.floor(100 / perSlide);
                    currentWidth = currentWidth - perWidth;
                }

                var blockHtml = '<div class="block" style="width: ' + perWidth + '%;" data-verified-type="{8}"> <div class="stamped-reviews-date">{7}</div> <div class="stamped-ratings-wrapper"><div class="stamped-reviews-rating stamped-style-color-star">{0}</div> <div class="stamped-reviews-title"><a href="{2}" class="stamped-reviews-link stamped-style-color-link">{1}</a></div> <div class="stamped-reviews-image"><a href="{2}" class="stamped-reviews-link"><img '+(isLazyLoad ? "data-":"")+'src="{3}" onerror="this.style.display=\'none\'" class="stamped-lazyload" alt="Product Image" /></a> <div class="stamped-reviews-product-title"> <a href="{2}" class="stamped-reviews-link stamped-style-color-link">{4}</a> </div> </div> <div class="stamped-reviews-message"> <div class="stamped-reviews-message-block stamped-style-color-text">{5}</div> </div> <div class="stamped-reviews-author stamped-style-color-text">{6}</div><div class="stamped-reviews-location stamped-style-color-text" style="display:none;">{9}</div> </div> </div> ';
                var starHtml = "";
                var rating = parseInt(arrData[i].reviewRating);
                while (rating > 0) {
                    starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star"></i>';
                    rating--;
                }

                var balance = (5 - parseInt(arrData[i].reviewRating));
                while (balance > 0) {
                    starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star-o"></i>';
                    balance--;
                }

                if (storeUrl) {
                    arrData[i].productUrl = arrData[i].productUrl + "?storeUrl=" + storeUrl;
                }

                if (arrData[i].productImageUrl) {
                    arrData[i].productImageUrl = arrData[i].productImageUrl.replace('%3F', '?');
                }

                stringBlocks += blockHtml.format(starHtml, arrData[i].reviewTitle, arrData[i].productUrl, arrData[i].productImageUrl, arrData[i].productName, arrData[i].reviewMessage, arrData[i].author, arrData[i].reviewDate, arrData[i].reviewVerifiedType, arrData[i].location);
            }

            $('.stamped-carousel-scroll-wrapper', obj).html(stringBlocks);

            $('.stamped-carousel-subtitle span.stamped-carousel-subtitle-count', obj).html(totalReviews);

            if (link) {

                var _html = $('.stamped-carousel-subtitle', obj).html();
                var _newLink = '<a href="' + link + '">' + _html + '</a>';

                $('.stamped-carousel-subtitle', obj).html(_newLink);
            }

            // set height
            if (isMobile || perSlide == 1) {
                // set height
                var getHeight = $('.block', obj).eq(0).height();

                // set height
                $('.stamped-carousel-scroll', obj).attr('style', 'height: ' + (getHeight + 50) + 'px');
            } else {
                var maxHeight = Math.max.apply(null, $('.block', obj).slice(0, 2).map(function () {
                    return $(this).height();
                }).get());

                // set height
                $('.stamped-carousel-scroll', obj).attr('style', 'height: ' + (maxHeight + 70) + 'px');
            }

            $(document).trigger('stamped:widget:carousel:loaded');

            if (autoPlay == true) {
                autoPlayInterval = setInterval(function () {
                    StampedCarouselFn.switchSlide(obj, 1);
                }, autoPlaySpeed);
            }

            var touchstartX = 0;
            var touchstartY = 0;
            var touchendX = 0;
            var touchendY = 0;

            $(obj)[0].addEventListener('touchstart', handleTouchStart2, false);
            $(obj)[0].addEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove2, false);

            var xDown = null;
            var yDown = null;

            function handleTouchStart2(evt) {
                var target = $(evt.target);
                if (!target.closest('#stamped-reviews-widget[data-widget-type="carousel"]').length) {
                    return;
                }
                xDown = evt.touches[0].clientX;
                yDown = evt.touches[0].clientY;
            };

            function handleTouchMove2(evt) {
                if (!xDown || !yDown) {
                    return;
                }

                var xUp = evt.touches[0].clientX;
                var yUp = evt.touches[0].clientY;

                var xDiff = xDown - xUp;
                var yDiff = yDown - yUp;

                if (Math.abs(xDiff) > Math.abs(yDiff)) {/*most significant*/
                    if (xDiff > 0) {
                        /* left swipe */
                        StampedCarouselFn.switchSlide(evt.target, 1, true);
                    } else {
                        /* right swipe */
                        StampedCarouselFn.switchSlide(evt.target, -1, true);
                    }
                } else {
                    if (yDiff > 0) {
                        /* up swipe */
                    } else {
                        /* down swipe */
                    }
                }
                /* reset values */
                xDown = null;
                yDown = null;
            };

            if (isLazyLoad) {
                LazyLoadStamped.update();
            }
        }

        function switchSlide(obj, pad, stopInterval) {
            if (stopInterval == true) {
                clearInterval(autoPlayInterval);
            }


            var stampedCarousel_currentPage = 1;
            var widget = $(obj).closest('#stamped-reviews-widget');
            var carousel = widget.find('#stamped-carousel-scroll-wrapper');

            perSlide = $(widget).data('per-slide') || 3;

            if ($(widget).attr('data-page')) {
                stampedCarousel_currentPage = parseInt($(widget).attr('data-page'));
            } else { }
            var totalBlocks = $('div[class=\"block\"]', carousel).length;
            var stampedCarousel_totalPage = totalBlocks > 0 ? Math.ceil(totalBlocks / perSlide) : 0;


            if (isMobile) {
                stampedCarousel_totalPage = totalBlocks;
            }
            if (stampedCarousel_currentPage == stampedCarousel_totalPage && pad == 1) {
                return;
            }
            stampedCarousel_currentPage += pad;
            if (stampedCarousel_currentPage == 0) {
                stampedCarousel_currentPage = 1;
            }
            $(widget).attr('data-page', stampedCarousel_currentPage);
            var width = carousel[0].offsetWidth * (stampedCarousel_currentPage - 1);
            carousel.attr('style', 'left: -' + width + 'px');


            if (isMobile || perSlide == 1) {
                // set height
                var getHeight = $('.block', widget).eq(stampedCarousel_currentPage - 1).height();

                // set height
                $('.stamped-carousel-scroll', widget).attr('style', 'height: ' + (getHeight + 50) + 'px');
            } else {
                var cIdx = (stampedCarousel_currentPage - 1) * perSlide;
                var nIdx = (stampedCarousel_currentPage) * perSlide;

                var maxHeight = Math.max.apply(null, $('.block', widget).slice(cIdx, nIdx).map(function () {
                    return $(this).height();
                }).get());

                // set height
                $('.stamped-carousel-scroll', widget).attr('style', 'height: ' + (maxHeight + 70) + 'px');
            }
        }

        String.prototype.format = function () {
            var formatted = this;
            for (var i = 0; i < arguments.length; i++) {
                var regexp = new RegExp('\\{' + i + '\\}', 'gi');
                formatted = formatted.replace(regexp, arguments[i]);
            }
            return formatted;
        };


        function shuffleArray(array) {
            for (var i = array.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                var j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
                var temp = array[i];
                array[i] = array[j];
                array[j] = temp;
            }

            return array;
        }

        return {
            init: init,
            switchSlide: switchSlide
        }
    }(window, document));
</script></div>

<script>
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
    function retinaImage(){
      $('img').each(function(){
        if($(this)[0].hasAttribute("data-retina-src") && window.devicePixelRatio > 1){
          if($(this)[0].hasAttribute("data-src")){
            $(this).attr("data-src",$(this).data("retina-src"));
            
          }else{
            $(this).attr("src",$(this).data("retina-src"));
          }
        }
      });
    }
    function isScrolledIntoView(element) {
      var scrollBottomPosition = $(window).scrollTop() + $(window).height();
      if ( $(element).is( "iframe" ) ) {
        return false;
      } else{
        return $(element).offset().top - 650 < scrollBottomPosition;
      }
    }
    function lazyload(element) {
      $(element).each(function() {
        if ($(this).hasClass("lazyloaded")){}else{
          if (isScrolledIntoView(this)) {
            var sorc = $(this).data("src");
            $(this).attr("src", sorc).one("load", function() {
              $(this).addClass('lazyloaded');
            });
          };
        };
      });
    }
    
    retinaImage();
    lazyload(".lazy");
    $(window).scroll(function() {
      lazyload(".lazy");
       
      $("iframe").each(function(){
        if($(this).hasClass("lazy")){
          if($(this).hasClass("lazyloaded")){}else{
          	var sorc = $(this).data("src");
            $(this).attr("src", sorc);
            $(this).addClass("lazyloaded");
          }
        }
      });
    });
    
    $(window).resize(function() {
      lazyload(".lazy");
    });
  });
</script>

<script>
window.addEventListener("load",function(){
 jQuery(function($) {
   var offset = 300;
   var duration = 500;
   $(window).scroll(function() {
     if ($(this).scrollTop() > offset) {
       $('.back-to-top').fadeIn(duration);
     } 
     else {
       $('.back-to-top').fadeOut(duration);
     }
   });
   $('.back-to-top').unbind('click.smoothscroll').bind('click', function(e) {
     e.preventDefault();
     $('html, body').animate( { scrollTop: 0 }, duration);
     return false;
   })
 });
});
</script>

<script>
    var StampedWidgetDrawerFn = (function (window, document) {
        var jQuery2;
        var _widgetData, _obj;

        function init(data, obj, $) {
            jQuery2 = $;
            var showAvatar = obj.data('show-avatar');
            var position = obj.data('position');
            var drawerText = obj.attr('data-text') || '';
            var drawerTitle = obj.attr('data-title') || '';
            var drawerLink = obj.attr('data-link') || '';
            var drawerLinkText = obj.attr('data-link-text') || 'View All Reviews';
            var isRandom = obj.data('random');
            var storeUrl = obj.attr('data-store-url');
            var isTotalWidget = obj.data('is-widget-total') == true ? true : false;

            if (isRandom == "true" || isRandom == "True") {
                data.data = shuffleArray(data.data);
            }

            var isLazyLoad = false;
            if (window.LazyLoadStamped && LazyLoadStamped.update) {
                isLazyLoad = true;
            }


            _widgetData = data.data;
            _obj = obj;


            /* STYLING */
            var classNameWidget = '#stamped-reviews-widget[data-widget-type="drawer"]';
            var styleColorStar = obj.data('style-color-star');
            var styleColorText = obj.data('style-color-text');
            var styleColorLink = obj.data('style-color-link');
            var drawerBackground = obj.data('background-color');
            var drawerTextColor = obj.data('style-color-button-text');


            var style = '';

            if (drawerBackground) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-widget-button { background:' + drawerBackground + ' !important; }';
            }

            if (drawerTextColor) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-widget-button { color:' + drawerTextColor + ' !important; }';
            }

            if (styleColorText) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-text { color:' + styleColorText + ' !important; }';
            }

            if (styleColorStar) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-star { color:' + styleColorStar + ' !important; }';
            }

            if (styleColorLink) {
                style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-link { color:' + styleColorLink + ' !important; }';
            }

            if (style) {
                jQuery2(obj).append('<style>' + style + '</style>');
            }
            /* ENDSTYLING */


            var totalAll = data.totalAll;

            if (data.totalAllWithNPS > 0) {
                totalAll = data.totalAllWithNPS;
            }

            if (isTotalWidget == true) {
                totalAll = data.total;
            }

            if (position == 'right') {
                var style = '<style>.stamped-widget-drawer { left: auto !important; right: -350px !important; transition: right 300ms cubic-bezier(0.17, 0.04, 0.03, 0.94) !important; } .stamped-widget-drawer .stamped-widget-button { margin-left: -44px !important; left: 0 !important; right: auto !important; } .stamped-widget-drawer.open{ right: 0 !important; left: auto !important; } @media only screen and (max-width: 480px), only screen and (max-device-width: 480px) { #stamped-widget-drawer { margin-top:100px !important; } }</style>';
                obj.append(style);
            }

            var drawer = jQuery2('.stamped-widget-drawer', obj);
            var drawer_content = jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-content', obj);
            var drawer_content_scroll = jQuery2('.scroll', obj);

            drawer_content_scroll.on('wheel', function (e) {
                var $this = jQuery2(this);
                if (e.originalEvent.deltaY < 0) {
                    /* scrolling up */
                    return ($this.scrollTop() > 0);
                } else {
                    /* scrolling down */
                    return ($this.scrollTop() + $this.innerHeight() < $this[0].scrollHeight);
                }
            });

            var drawer_button = jQuery2('.stamped-widget-button');
            drawer_button.on('click', function () {

                var d = jQuery2("#stamped-widget-drawer");

                var state = jQuery2(d).hasClass('open');

                if (!state) {
                    d.addClass("open");
                    _obj.addClass("open");
                } else {
                    d.removeClass("open");
                    _obj.removeClass("open");
                }
            });

            jQuery2(document).on('click', '.stamped-drawer-content, .stamped-widget-button, #stamped-reviews-widget', function (e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
            });

            var documentClick;
            jQuery2(document).on('touchstart', function () {
                documentClick = true;
            });
            jQuery2(document).on('touchmove', function () {
                documentClick = false;
            });
            jQuery2(document).on('click touchend', function (e) {
                if (e.type === "click") documentClick = true;
                if (documentClick) {
                    //console.log(e.target);
                    if (e.target.id === "stamped-widget-drawer" || jQuery2(e.target).parents("#stamped-widget-drawer").length) {
                        //console.log("Inside div");
                    } else {
                        //console.log("Outside div");

                        setTimeout(function () {
                            var d = jQuery2("#stamped-widget-drawer");

                            var state = jQuery2(d).hasClass('open');

                            if (state) {
                                d.removeClass("open");
                                _obj.removeClass("open");
                            }
                        }, 300);
                    }
                }
            });

            if (drawerText) {
                jQuery2('.vertical-text').html(drawerText);
            }

            if (drawerTitle != '' && drawerTitle) {
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-title').html(drawerTitle);
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-title').attr('data-count', totalAll);

                jQuery2(drawer).append('<style>.stamped-drawer-title:before { content: " " !important; }</style>');
            } else {
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-title').attr('data-count', totalAll);
            }

            if (drawerLink) {
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-link-wrapper').show();
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-link-wrapper').css('display', 'block');
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-link-text').html(drawerLinkText);
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-link').attr('href', drawerLink);
            }

            var totalTake = 60;
            var totalLength = data.data.length >= totalTake ? totalTake : data.data.length;

            var productReviewsWrapper = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-drawer-product-reviews"></div>');
            var siteReviewsWrapper = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-drawer-site-reviews" style="display:none;"></div>');

            var isSiteReview = false;
            var indexWithPhotos = 0;

            for (var i = 0; i < totalLength; i++) {
                if (data.data[i].reviewRating) {
                    var stars = "";

                    if (data.data[i].reviewType == 1) {
                        var rating = parseInt(data.data[i].reviewRating);
                        while (rating > 0) {
                            stars += '<i class="fa fa-star stamped-style-color-star"></i>';
                            rating--;
                        }
                    } else if (data.data[i].reviewType == 2) {
                        stars = '<i class="fa fa-smile-o"></i>';
                        isSiteReview = true;
                    }

                    var ratingWrapper = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-ratings-wrapper stamped-review-card"></div>');
                    var rating = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-reviews-rating"> ' + stars + '</div>');
                    var message = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-reviews-message"></div>');
                    var messageBlock = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-reviews-message-block stamped-style-color-text"></div>').html(data.data[i].reviewMessage);

                    var imageBlock = null;
                    if (data.data[i].reviewUserPhotos) {
                        var imageTags = "";
                        for (var ii = 0; ii < data.data[i].reviewUserPhotos.split(',').length; ii++) {
                            var _src = data.data[i].reviewUserPhotos.split(',')[ii];
                            var _photoThumbSrc = 'https://d2yz4gcx05ko3u.cloudfront.net/uploads/photos/thumb/' + _src;
                            var _photoFullSrc = 'https://d2yz4gcx05ko3u.cloudfront.net/uploads/photos/' + _src;
                            imageTags += "<a onclick='StampedWidgetDrawerFn.openModal(" + indexWithPhotos + ", " + ii + ");' style='cursor:pointer;'><img " + (isLazyLoad == true ? "data-" : "") + "src='" + _photoThumbSrc + "' class='stamped-lazyload' height='50' style='margin-right:5px;' /></a>";
                        }

                        indexWithPhotos++;

                        imageBlock = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-reviews-message-image-block">' + imageTags + '</div>');
                    }

                    if (storeUrl) {
                        data.data[i].productUrl = data.data[i].productUrl + "?storeUrl=" + storeUrl;
                    }

                    //var title = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-reviews-title"> ' + data[i].reviewTitle + '</div>');
                    var author = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-reviews-author"> ' + data.data[i].author + '</div>');
                    var date = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-reviews-date"> ' + data.data[i].reviewDate + '</div>');
                    var reviewTitle = jQuery2('<div class="stamped-reviews-title"><a href="' + data.data[i].productUrl + '" class="stamped-style-color-link">' + (data.data[i].reviewTitle || '') + '</a>');

                    if (showAvatar == true || showAvatar == 'True') {
                        var productImage = jQuery2('<div style="height: 60px; width:50px; float:left; margin-right:10px; display: inline-block; vertical-align: top; text-align:center; position: relative; padding-left:0 !important; z-index:9;" class="stamped-review-image"><a href="' +
                            data.data[i].productUrl + '"><img ' + (isLazyLoad == true ? "data-" : "") + 'src="' +
                            data.data[i].productImageUrl + '" class="stamped-lazyload" style="max-height: 100%; max-width: 100%; width: auto; height: auto; top: 0; bottom: 0; left: 0; right: 0; margin: auto;" onerror="this.style.display=\'none\'"></a></div>')
                        if (data.data[i].productImageUrl) {
                            ratingWrapper.append(productImage);
                        }
                    }

                    ratingWrapper.append(rating);

                    ratingWrapper.append(date);
                    ratingWrapper.append(author);
                    if (data.data[i].reviewTitle) {
                        ratingWrapper.append(reviewTitle);
                    }
                    message.append(messageBlock);
                    if (imageBlock) {
                        message.append(imageBlock);
                    }
                    ratingWrapper.append(message);

                    if (data.data[i].reviewType == 1) {
                        productReviewsWrapper.append(ratingWrapper);
                    } else if (data.data[i].reviewType == 2) {
                        siteReviewsWrapper.append(ratingWrapper);
                    }
                }
            }

            drawer_content_scroll.append(productReviewsWrapper);
            drawer_content_scroll.append(siteReviewsWrapper);

            // check if widget already exists
            if (!jQuery2('#stamped-widget-drawer').length) {
                jQuery2('body').append(drawer);
            }

            // check if contains site review, show tabs
            if (isSiteReview) {
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-tabs').show();
            }

            jQuery2('.stamped-ratings-wrapper', obj).each(function (index) {
                var _obj = jQuery2('.stamped-reviews-message', this).find('.stamped-reviews-message-block');
                if (_obj.length) {
                    var txtOriginal = jQuery2(_obj).html();
                    if (txtOriginal.length > 300) {
                        var txtTrimmed = txtOriginal.substring(0, 300) // get first 600 characters
                            .split(" ") // separate characters into an array of words
                            .slice(0, -1) // remove the last full or partial word
                            .join(" ") + "..."; // combine into a single string and append "..."
                        _obj.html(txtTrimmed);

                        _obj.append('<a class="stamped-drawer-read-more" style="margin-left:10px; cursor:pointer;"></span>');
                        _obj.append('<span class="original-review" style="display:none !important;">' + txtOriginal + '</span>');
                    }
                }
            });

            jQuery2(document).on('click', '.stamped-drawer-read-more', function () {
                // remove link
                // replace text
                jQuery2(this).closest('.stamped-reviews-message-block').find('.read-more').hide();
                var txtOriginal = jQuery2(this).closest('.stamped-reviews-message-block').find('.original-review').html();

                jQuery2(this).closest('.stamped-reviews-message-block').html(txtOriginal);
            });

            if (isLazyLoad) {
                LazyLoadStamped.update();
            }
        }

        var switchTab = function (type) {
            if (jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-tabs li#product-reviews').length) {
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-tabs li#product-reviews').removeClass('active');
            }

            if (jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-tabs li#site-reviews').length) {
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-tabs li#site-reviews').removeClass('active');
            }

            jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-product-reviews').hide();
            jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-site-reviews').hide();

            jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-tabs li#' + type).addClass('active');

            if (type == 'product-reviews') {
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-product-reviews').show();
            } else {
                jQuery2('.stamped-drawer-site-reviews').show();
            }
        }

        function openModal(index, idxPhoto) {
            StampedFn.openUGCModal(_widgetData, index, _obj, 1, true, idxPhoto);
        }


        function shuffleArray(array) {
            for (var i = array.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                var j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
                var temp = array[i];
                array[i] = array[j];
                array[j] = temp;
            }

            return array;
        }

        return {
            init: init,
            switchTab: switchTab,
            openModal: openModal
        }
    }(window, document));
